<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN被广泛用于空间图结构的多变量时间序列短期预测，但方法创新大多仅在一小部分基准数据集上评估。研究发现这些数据集上简单的线性基线模型往往与GNN表现相当，这引发了对基准数据集判别能力和可靠性的质疑。作者希望通过经典时间序列分析来深入探究这些数据集的内在特征，以揭示为什么空间无关的线性模型是比以往报道更强的竞争者，并帮助社区识别数据集的潜在偏差。

Method: 本文采用经典时间序列分析方法对多种基准数据集进行系统统计分析。具体包括：利用统计工具集识别数据集中的显著空间和时间相关性；分析一阶差分数据集引入的结构性偏差；对比空间无关线性模型（如历史平均和经典机器学习方法）与GNN的性能差距。基于分析结果，提出一个简单的混合模型，将统计洞察融入GNN开发中。

Result: 研究表明：1）这些广泛采用的基准数据集中存在显著的结构性偏差，尤其是一阶差分处理引入的偏差；2）空间无关的线性模型在这些数据集上的竞争力被严重低估；3）提供了一套统计工具集用于识别数据集中的空间和时间相关性；4）通过一个简单混合模型展示了将统计分析方法融入GNN模型开发的新思路。

Conclusion: 作者建议减少对这些广泛采用数据集的过度依赖，转而倡导更严格的统计评估方法。本文的方法论不仅能帮助揭示现有基准数据集的局限，还能为开发新型GNN模型提供指导，推动该领域向更可靠的评估和实践方向发展。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are routinely employed for short-range forecasting on multivariate time series with a spatial graph structure. Despite the availability of many alternative datasets, method innovations within this domain are predominantly assessed against a rather limited set of benchmark datasets, most notably Chickenpox, PedalMe, WikiMaths, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY. The evaluation protocols contain baselines spanning from historical averages to classical machine learning approaches. These baselines often show competitive performance compared to GNNs. In the present work, we take a step back and analyse the benchmark datasets via classical time series methods to uncover why spatially-unaware linear models pose a stronger competitor than previously reported, casting further doubt on the discriminative reliability of the aforementioned widely adopted datasets. Our statistical analysis provides a toolset for identifying significant spatial and temporal correlations, while revealing a structural bias introduced by first-order differenced datasets. We therefore recommend reducing the over-reliance on such datasets for method comparison, and instead advocate for more rigorous statistical evaluation. By applying the results of our analysis to a simple hybrid model, we show how our methodology can lead to novel ways of developing GNN models

</details>


### [36] [Jacobian-guided Noise Injection for Quantization Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20988)
*Deepanshu Pandey,Arnav Chavan,Nahush Lele,Sankalp Dayal,Deepak Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jacobian引导的噪声注入训练策略，通过在注意力logits中注入方差由Jacobian Frobenius范数推导的零均值高斯噪声，来抑制softmax算子的Jacobian范数，从而提升大语言模型在低比特量化下的鲁棒性。在多种先进LLM架构上，该方法相比主流PTQ方法在ImageNet-1K上Top-1准确率相对提升最高达37%，在WikiText上困惑度相对改善最高达40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型量化常受限于自注意力机制对离散化误差的敏感性。作者识别出softmax算子因对异常值敏感且具有状态依赖的Jacobian，成为量化稳定性的瓶颈，需要一种有理论依据的方法来抑制量化引发的性能退化。

Method: 1) 理论层面证明抑制softmax Jacobian的范数有助于界定量化引发的性能退化；2) 提出Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection (JGNI)训练策略，在注意力logits中注入零均值高斯噪声，其方差直接从Jacobian Frobenius范数推导得出；3) 与依赖启发式或直接惩罚Jacobian的先前方法不同，该方法可基于局部注意力敏感性识别最优噪声方差。

Result: 在SOTA LLM架构上，JGNI相比主流PTQ（后训练量化）方法展示出更强的鲁棒性。具体而言，在ImageNet-1K上SigLIP的Top-1准确率相对提升最高达+37%，在WikiText上语言模型低比特量化的相对困惑度改善最高达40%。

Conclusion: 通过理论驱动的噪声注入策略有效抑制softmax Jacobian范数，能够显著提升大语言模型在量化条件下的性能鲁棒性，为低比特量化场景下的模型训练提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often hindered by the sensitivity of the self-attention mechanism to discretization errors. We identify the softmax operator as a bottleneck for quantization stability due to its sensitivity to outliers and state-dependent Jacobian. We theoretically establish that suppressing the norm of this Jacobian helps in bounding quantization-induced performance degradation. Based on this, we propose Jacobian-Guided Noise Injection, a training strategy that injects zero-mean Gaussian noise into pre-attention logits, with variance derived directly from the Jacobian Frobenius norm. Unlike prior approaches that rely on heuristic or penalise jacobian directly, our method provides a way to identify the optimal noise variance based on the local attention sensitivity. We evaluate the method on SOTA LLM architectures, where it demonstrates improved robustness over popular PTQ methods. Empirical analysis reveals that the proposed method gives up to +37% relative gains on Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K for SigLIP and improves relative perplexity by upto 40% on WikiText for language models in low bit quantisation settings, proving the efficacy of the approach.

</details>


### [37] [Trojaning the Alignment: Stealthy Backdoor Attacks against Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20991)
*Minhua Lin,Zhicheng Gao,Yilong Wang,Hanqing Lu,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了STAG，一种针对文本属性图上图基础模型的隐蔽木马攻击框架，通过在图-语言对齐接口协调图触发器生成与文本侧软提示，使触发器附加的图表示和文本表示向相同目标类别文本区域移动，并利用候选检索生成可读触发器节点文本和正则化局部子图结构来保证隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于图基础模型的后门攻击研究不足，尤其是在图-语言对齐场景下。已有的攻击要么只针对图侧，要么只针对文本侧，将两种模态独立处理，导致直改不适应：图侧触发器会受到干净文本语义的约束，文本侧触发器改变语言视角但不能直接移动被对齐和评分的图表示。此外，TAG中触发器同时暴露为节点文本和局部图结构，不连贯的触发器属性或异常的次图容易被检测和过滤，隐蔽性挑战突出。

Method: STAG框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 图触发器生成器与文本侧软提示的协同，使触发器附加的图表示与触发文本表示共同向目标类别的文本区域收敛；(2) 通过候选检索实现触发器节点的可读文本，并对触发器附加的次图进行正则化，使其局部结构保持与原始次图接近，以满足TAG特有的隐蔽性要求。

Result: 在多个TAG数据集和代表性GFM上的大量实验验证了STAG的有效性和隐蔽性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: STAG成功填补了GFM在TAG上后门攻击研究的空白，针对图-语言对齐接口设计攻击，并专门解决了TAG场景下的隐蔽性挑战，为理解GFM的安全脆弱性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) align graph representations with language semantics to support transferable graph learning. Despite these advantages, the backdoor vulnerability of GFMs on TAGs remains insufficiently understood, especially under graph-language alignment, where graph and text representations are trained to constrain each other in a shared semantic space. Existing backdoor attacks mainly target either the graph side or the text side, treating the two modalities independently. This makes direct adaptation ineffective: graph-only triggers can be constrained by clean text semantics, while text-only triggers alter the language view but do not directly shift the graph representation being aligned and scored. TAGs also impose a stealth challenge because triggers are exposed as both node text and local graph structure, making incoherent trigger attributes or anomalous subgraphs easy to inspect or filter. In this paper, we propose STAG, a stealthy trojan attack framework designed for the graph-language alignment interface of GFMs on TAGs. STAG coordinates a graph-trigger generator with a text-side soft prompt so that trigger-attached graph representations and triggered text representations move toward the same target-class text region. To address TAG-specific stealthiness, STAG realizes trigger nodes as readable text through candidate retrieval and regularizes the trigger-attached subgraph so that its local structure remains close to the original subgraph. Extensive experiments on multiple TAG datasets and representative GFMs demonstrate the effectiveness and stealthiness of STAG. Our code is available at https://github.com/ventr1c/STAG.

</details>


### [38] [Free-Probability Kernels for Zero-Rollout Hyperparameter Selection in Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20998)
*Sara Malacarne,Andrea Ceni,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自由概率的确定性核方法，用于在无需实例化或滚动运行储层计算（RC）系统的情况下选择超参数（递归增益、输入尺度、泄漏率）。通过自由概率计算交叉滞后传播系数，定义大宽度极限下的确定性时间核，并在短标注引导序列上进行核岭回归以排序候选运行状态。在十个合成时间序列基准上，零滚动选择达到0.772的平均部署得分（接近穷举搜索的0.774），同时避免了156,600次选择滚动；在ETT和蜂窝流量预测等实际数据集上也达到或接近穷举参考性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算的核心优势在于固定循环动态系统加轻量读出的高效性，但超参数选择过程（递归增益、输入尺度、泄漏率）通常需要大量滚动来调节储层的稳定性和时间处理模式，这严重削弱了其效率优势。现有方法依赖大量验证滚动，在验证滚动稀缺的场景下难以有效选择运行状态。

Method: 提出一种确定性的、基于引导（pilot）信息的超参数选择器，适用于泄漏线性储层加逐坐标非线性特征的结构。利用自由概率理论推导交叉滞后传播系数，这些系数概括了储层对过去输入的混合方式；在大宽度极限下，这些系数定义了一个确定性时间核，逼近有限储层的特征几何。随后在短标注引导序列上执行核岭回归以排序候选运行状态，无需实例化或滚动运行储层，且所选配置可跨宽度迁移。

Result: 在十个合成时间序列基准上，零滚动选择获得0.772的平均部署得分，穷举模拟搜索为0.774，同时节省了156,600次选择滚动。在小滚动预算下，所提排序方法在每个测试预算下均取得最强平均性能，并以穷举参考4.8%的滚动成本达到参考水平。在四个ETT数据集上，保留五个候选可在三个数据集上恢复穷举工作点；在多元蜂窝流量预测上，每单元15次滚动即可达到462次滚动的穷举参考水平，并在低预算下优于随机搜索和贝叶斯优化。

Conclusion: 自由概率核可作为确定性替代模型，用于在验证滚动稀缺的情况下选择储层运行状态。该方法实现了接近穷举搜索的性能而几乎无需滚动成本，为储层计算的超参数选择提供了高效且理论上可解释的工具。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) couples a fixed recurrent dynamical system with a trained lightweight readout, but this efficiency is partly lost during hyperparameter selection: the recurrent gain, input scale, and leakage rate determine the reservoir's stability and temporal processing regime and are usually tuned through many rollouts. We introduce a deterministic, pilot-informed selector for leaky linear reservoirs followed by coordinate-wise nonlinear features. Free probability yields cross-lag propagation coefficients that summarize how the reservoir mixes past inputs. In the large-width limit, these coefficients define a deterministic temporal kernel that approximates the finite-reservoir feature geometry. Kernel ridge regression on a short labelled pilot sequence therefore ranks candidate operating regimes without instantiating or rolling out a reservoir, and the selected configuration transfers across widths. Across ten synthetic temporal benchmarks, zero-rollout selection obtains a mean deployment score of $0.772$, compared with $0.774$ for exhaustive simulation-based search, while avoiding $156\,600$ selection rollouts. With a small rollout budget, the proposed ranking provides the strongest mean performance at every tested budget and reaches the exhaustive reference using $4.8\%$ of its rollout cost. On four public electricity-transformer-temperature (ETT) forecasting datasets, five retained candidates recover the exhaustive operating point on three datasets. On multivariate cellular-traffic forecasting, 15 rollouts per cell reach the 462-rollout exhaustive reference and outperform random search and Bayesian optimization at low budgets. These results position free-probability kernels as deterministic surrogates for selecting reservoir operating regimes when validation rollouts are scarce.

</details>


### [39] [RODE: A Radial-Orthogonal Decoupled Engine for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21024)
*Guoxiang Xu,Bince Qu,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RODE优化器，将矩阵权重的径向（范数）和方向性更新解耦，分别采用独立的更新规则和步长。RODE通过标量径向规则显式更新矩阵Frobenius范数，方向性通道在切空间中执行Newton-Schulz条件化更新。在GPT-2干预实验、两个语言建模和两个图像分类任务中，RODE均优于Muon变体；在1.5B规模下将损失从4.145降至3.346，在Qwen3.5-9B全参数微调中所有评估任务上均超越Muon。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络训练广泛使用矩阵感知优化器，但其条件化矩阵步通常直接加到权重上，会同时改变权重的范数和方向。这存在问题：当前范数决定角向运动的大小，而方向性学习又能驱动范数增长从而改变后续步骤，两者耦合在一起难以独立控制，导致优化行为不可控且效率受限。

Method: RODE将矩阵更新的径向和方向性分量解耦：径向通道通过标量规则显式更新矩阵的Frobenius范数，方向性通道在切空间中执行Newton-Schulz条件化更新。两者具有独立的更新规则和步长，从而实现对矩阵优化的精细控制。

Result: 受控GPT-2干预实验表明直接范数控制和RODE方向性更新均有效。跨两个语言建模和两个图像分类任务，RODE在每次直接比较中均优于两种Muon变体且最终全模型范数更低。在1.5B规模下，RODE将损失从4.145降至3.346，全局范数从11964降至2183，固定半径RODE进一步提升。在Qwen3.5-9B全参数微调中，RODE在全部四个评估任务上超越两种Muon变体，并在GSM8K和MATH-500上取得最高均值。

Conclusion: 解耦径向与方向性动力学为矩阵优化提供了一种更有效且更可控的方法，验证了显式范数控制与条件化方向更新的协同价值。

Abstract: Modern neural network training increasingly uses matrix-aware optimizers, yet their conditioned matrix step is typically added directly to the weight, jointly changing its norm and direction. This interaction matters because the current norm determines angular motion, while directional learning can drive norm growth and thereby alter later steps. We introduce RODE, which gives the radial and directional components separate update rules and step sizes. RODE explicitly updates the matrix Frobenius norm through a scalar radial rule, while its directional channel performs Newton--Schulz-conditioned updates in the tangent space. Controlled GPT-2 interventions show gains from both direct norm control and RODE's directional update. Across two language-modeling and two image-classification tasks, RODE outperforms both Muon variants in every direct comparison and ends with lower full-model norms. At 1.5B scale, using the learning rate transferred directly from the Qwen2-style LM sweep, RODE lowers loss from 4.145 to 3.346 and final global norm from 11964 to 2183 relative to Muon RMS, with fixed-radius RODE improving further. For Qwen3.5-9B full-parameter fine-tuning, all six optimizers use the same tuning budget and the same formal-training and evaluation settings; RODE outperforms both Muon variants on all four evaluation tasks and attains the highest mean on GSM8K and MATH-500. Thus, decoupling radial and directional dynamics offers a more effective and controllable approach to matrix optimization.

</details>


### [40] [Designing a Robust LLM-Based Evaluation System for Agentic AI in Drug Discovery Through Human Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21057)
*Emma Granqvist,Rocío Mercado,Samuel Genheden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评测代理式LLM药物发现系统ChatInvent的LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架，通过定义四个质量维度、人类对齐验证、少样本优化和held-out测试四个贡献，实现了与人类专家高度对齐的自动化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式LLM系统在化学和药物发现领域正在重塑科研工作流，但对其开放式、工具增强输出的评估仍是根本瓶颈：基于参考的指标（BLEU、ROUGE）无法捕捉语义正确性，而人工专家评估无法扩展到系统迭代所需的规模。LLM-as-a-Judge范式虽已出现，但现有药物发现基准并未验证LLM法官与人类专家的对齐程度。

Method: 1) 定义了四个输出质量评估维度（完整性、相关性、结构清晰度、范围遵循）以及确定性的工具调用正确性检查；2) 通过五名专家标注员的人类对齐研究，对比Gemini 3.1 Pro、Claude Opus 4.7、GPT-5和Llama 3.1 70B四个候选法官；3) 使用人类标注的少样本示例优化最佳法官；4) 将优化后的法官应用于70个留出问题以发现系统局限性。

Result: 少样本优化将法官与人类多数投票的对齐度从0.80提升到0.86；在70个留出问题上发现非正式措辞并不会系统性降低输出质量，反而让LLM重写原始问题后再查询agent是有帮助的。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学领域中代理式系统的人类对齐自动化评估提供了可复用模板。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are reshaping scientific workflows in chemistry and drug discovery, but evaluating their open-ended, tool-augmented outputs remains a fundamental bottleneck. Reference-based metrics such as BLEU and ROUGE fail to capture semantic correctness, while expert human evaluation does not scale to the iteration speed these systems demand. The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative, but existing drug discovery benchmarks deploy LLM judges without validating their alignment with human experts. In this work, we present an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation framework for ChatInvent, an agentic drug discovery assistant deployed at AstraZeneca, with four contributions. First, we define four output-quality evaluation dimensions---Completeness, Relevancy, Structural Clarity, and Scope Adherence---alongside deterministic Tool Call Correctness checks. Second, we validate the judge through a human alignment study with five expert annotators, comparing Gemini 3.1 Pro, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, and Llama 3.1 70B as candidate judges. Third, we optimize the best-performing judge using few-shot demonstrations of human-annotated examples, improving alignment with the human majority vote from 0.80 to 0.86. Fourth, applying the optimized judge to 70 held-out questions, we surface concrete limitations and find that informal phrasings do not systematically degrade output quality; if anything, it is helpful to have the LLM rewrite the original question before querying the agent. Our framework provides a reusable template for human-aligned evaluation of agentic systems in scientific domains.

</details>


### [41] [TracingFlow: A Simulation-Free Trajectory Inference Framework Based on Second-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21070)
*Yuhao Sun,Zekun Wu,Zixun Huang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TracingFlow是一种模拟自由的Flow Matching框架，通过神经网络回归加速度场来推广至二阶动力学，精确高效地求解Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT)问题，克服了一阶最优传输方法在稀疏时间快照中推断连续系统演化时的表达力限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输框架局限于一阶动力学，假设无记忆速度场，无法捕捉细胞分化等过程中存在的调控动量和时间延迟响应。一阶方法会产生过度平滑的轨迹，缺乏对高曲率转变和非线性演化的建模能力，因此需要二阶动力学框架以提升系统演化推断的表达力。

Method: TracingFlow采用模拟自由的Flow Matching方法，通过神经网络学习并回归加速度场（force field），将最优传输问题推广到二阶动力学，精确且高效地求解Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT)问题，并整合谱系追踪先验以恢复数学最优且生物合理的动力学结构。

Result: 在复杂合成数据集和大规模scRNA-seq数据集上，TracingFlow在分布重建精度和轨迹忠实度方面均优于现有方法，验证了二阶公式在捕捉高曲率转变和非线性演化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: TracingFlow通过二阶动力学扩展解决了稀疏时间快照下连续系统演化推断的瓶颈问题，不仅提升了轨迹预测的准确性，还通过整合生物先验保证了结果兼具数学最优性与生物学可解释性。

Abstract: Inferring continuous system evolution from sparse temporal snapshots is a key challenge in generative modeling and single-cell omics. While Optimal Transport (OT) is popular, existing frameworks are largely restricted to first-order dynamics, assuming memoryless velocity fields. This limits expressiveness, as first-order systems fail to account for regulatory momentum and time-delayed responses inherent in processes like cell differentiation. Here, we introduce TracingFlow, a simulation-free Flow Matching framework generalizing to second-order dynamics. By using neural networks to regress the acceleration field, TracingFlow provides an exact, efficient solution to the Dynamical Optimal Acceleration Transport (DOAT) problem. Unlike first-order methods yielding over-smoothed trajectories, our second-order formulation captures high-curvature transitions and nonlinear evolutions by learning the underlying force fields. Evaluated on complex synthetic and large-scale scRNA-seq datasets, TracingFlow achieves superior accuracy in distributional reconstruction and trajectory faithfulness. Moreover, by integrating lineage tracing priors, it recovers dynamical structures that are both mathematically optimal and biologically plausible.

</details>


### [42] [Causal Modeling of Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes via Adaptive LLM Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21079)
*Kavimayil P. Komarasamy,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,David M. Haas,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号框架，用于生成合理的因果假设。该框架迭代地结合大型语言模型的广泛先验知识与数据的经验评分，将LLM视为自适应提议分布，生成假设并对经验数据进行评分，然后用高评分图更新LLM上下文，引导后续生成向更优假设空间探索。在真实临床数据APOs建模中，该方法恢复了所有专家验证的因果关系边，并发现了专家未列出的额外合理因果关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不良妊娠结局（APOs）如早产和妊娠糖尿病对母婴都有长期后果，但其病因理解仍不清楚。该领域的因果发现尤其困难，因为数据匮乏且领域知识不完整，导致纯数据驱动方法失败，而LLM输出不一致或矛盾。需要一个能结合LLM先验知识和经验数据评分的框架。

Method: 提出一个神经符号框架：将LLM作为自适应提议分布，迭代生成因果假设（图），对生成的图基于经验数据进行评分，将高评分图作为上下文反馈给LLM，引导后续生成朝向更优假设空间。该过程形成"生成-评分-更新上下文"的迭代循环。

Result: 在真实临床数据集上评估APOs及其风险因素建模，与专家构建的因果图对比：该方法恢复了所有专家验证的边，并识别出额外的、此前专家未列出的合理因果关系，可能为靶向干预提供新见解。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经符号框架能有效结合LLM的先验知识与数据经验评分，在数据匮乏和领域知识不完整的场景下生成优于纯数据驱动和纯LLM方法的因果假设，有望为APOs的因果发现提供新视角。

Abstract: Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes (APOs) such as preterm birth and gestational diabetes can have long-term consequences for both the mother and child, yet an understanding of their causes remains elusive. Causal discovery in this domain is especially challenging due to a paucity of data and incomplete domain knowledge. As a result, pure data-driven methods fail, and Large Language Model (LLM) outputs remain inconsistent or contradictory. We introduce a neurosymbolic framework for generating plausible causal hypotheses that iteratively combines the broad prior knowledge of LLMs with empirical scoring on data. Our method treats the LLM as an adaptive proposal distribution, generating hypotheses that are scored against empirical data; the resulting high-scoring graphs are then used to update the LLM's context, steering subsequent generations toward more promising regions of the hypothesis space. We evaluate our approach on a real-world clinical dataset for modeling APOs and their risk factors, comparing our results against an expert-constructed causal graph. Our method recovers all expert-validated edges and identifies additional plausible causal relations not previously listed by experts, potentially providing new insights for targeted interventions.

</details>


### [43] [FlatLand: Personalized Graph Federated Learning via Tailored Lorentz Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21096)
*Jiahong Liu,Ram Samarth B B,Xinyu Fu,Menglin Yang,Weixi Zhang,Rex Ying,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlatLand，一种基于双曲几何Lorentz空间的个性化联邦图学习方法，通过将不同客户端数据嵌入定制化的Lorentz空间，并采用参数解耦策略分离异质性信息与公共知识，无需客户端相似度估计即可实现直接聚合，在低维设置下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习（PFL）方法忽视了图结构的内在几何特性，无法有效处理客户端之间高度异构的图数据。由于真实世界的图普遍具有负曲率特性，而当前方法缺乏对此类几何结构的建模能力，因此需要一种能够利用图的双曲几何性质并同时处理客户端异质性的联邦学习方法。

Method: FlatLand将不同客户端的数据嵌入到定制化的Lorentz空间（双曲几何）中。核心思想包括：(1) 利用双曲几何自然适配真实世界图的负曲率特性；(2) 利用Lorentz空间中的类时（time-like）维度编码客户端特定的异质性信息；(3) 采用参数解耦策略，将异质性信息捕获在类时参数中，将公共知识保留在类空（space-like）参数中，从而实现无需客户端相似度估计和额外计算模块的直接聚合。

Result: 在多种联邦图学习任务上的实验结果表明，FlatLand取得了优于现有方法的性能，特别是在低维嵌入设置下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: FlatLand证明双曲几何（Lorentz空间）为联邦图学习中的客户端异质性提供了一种原理性且有效的建模方式，参数解耦策略使得个性化联邦学习更加简洁高效，无需依赖客户端相似度估计等额外机制。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but highly heterogeneous client data remain challenging, especially in graph federated learning where clients possess structurally diverse graphs. Existing personalized federated learning (PFL) methods ignore the intrinsic geometric properties of diverse graph structures. We propose FlatLand, a novel personalized federated learning method that embeds different clients' data in tailored Lorentz space of hyperbolic geometry. Our key insight is that hyperbolic geometry naturally accommodates the intrinsic negative curvature prevalent in real-world graphs, while the time-like dimension in Lorentz space provides a principled way to encode client-specific heterogeneity. We develop a parameter decoupling strategy that separates heterogeneous information (captured in time-like parameters) from common knowledge (preserved in space-like parameters), enabling direct aggregation without requiring client similarity estimation and extra calculation modules. Empirical results on diverse federated graph learning tasks demonstrate that FlatLand achieves superior performance, particularly in low-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [44] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COEC是一种无需训练的LLM结构化剪枝补偿框架，通过对保留权重施加交替左右正交旋转并在Stiefel流形上优化右旋转、使用广义交叉验证选择正则强度、温度化校准Gram矩阵并引入对齐惩罚来减少剪枝输出误差，在Llama-3/3.1/Qwen2.5家族上一致优于现有补偿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的剪枝补偿方法仅使用加法偏置或单一正交旋转作用于保留权重的输出侧，这些修正不改变保留权重的输入奇异框架，从而限制了列移除后保留权重的适应能力，导致补偿效果有限。

Method: COEC框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 对保留权重施加交替的左和右正交旋转；(2) 在简化Stiefel流形上优化右旋转；(3) 使用广义交叉验证(GCV)逐层选择奇异值重缩放的正则化强度；(4) 温度化(调温)校准Gram矩阵以降低高能激活方向的支配性；(5) 引入对齐惩罚保持相邻注意力投影间的几何关系。所有组件仅使用小校准集的二阶统计量，无需反向传播或模型重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5模型家族、多种结构化稀疏度下的实验表明，COEC在每个模型上均改善了困惑度(PPL)，在大多数设置中提升了零样本准确率，且在高稀疏度下增益更大。COEC独立于列剪枝准则，可应用于多种结构化剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 后剪枝补偿能够恢复部分因列移除导致的性能损失，COEC通过更灵活的左右正交旋转补偿机制在与在压缩/精度权衡中取得了最优效果。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [45] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Winder，一种利用心脏周期相移等变性的自监督表示学习架构，通过将表征组织为相不变坐标和相位旋转谐波子空间来编码生理周期性，在PTB-XL上以约1M参数达到SOTA水平诊断精度，同时保持相位等变的潜在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的周期结构为自监督表征学习提供了天然先验，但现有方法未充分利用这种对称性。心脏周期是一个定义良好的周期性生理过程，显式编码其相位对称性有望保留诊断信息并产生更具可解释性的潜在空间。

Method: 推导了相位等变的自监督目标函数，并引入Winder联合嵌入架构，将表征组织为相位不变坐标和相位旋转谐波子空间。其传输算子是固定的闭式解，源于周期几何而非学习得到，不引入额外参数。

Result: 在PTB-XL数据集上采用冻结线性探针协议评估，Winder以约1M参数达到SOTA自监督方法的诊断准确率范围，同时展现出相位等变的潜在几何结构，表明显式编码心脏相位对称性既能保留诊断信息又能产生可读且参数高效的潜在空间。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性是有效的自监督学习策略，能够在保持诊断性能的同时获得参数高效、可直接关联到可测量生理量的潜在几何表征。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [46] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPIN，一个神经符号框架，用于从非结构化临床叙述中诱导概率规划领域模型（PPDDL）。该框架利用预训练LLM从原始文本中提取和补齐结构化事件序列，并迭代地用LLM提出的修订来精化模型前置条件，通过实证验证引导。在9位外科医生的2,660份腹腔镜阑尾切除手术记录上评估，模型能泛化到未见记录，且专家临床审查表明诱导的知识与手术实践高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜阑尾切除术等外科手术是复杂且高风险的过程，但其工作流的正式化（用于决策支持）非常困难。主要原因包括：缺乏结构化事件数据，以及临床叙述中普遍存在隐含动作。纯符号方法或纯LLM方法都无法单独有效解决这些问题。

Method: NSPIN采用神经符号混合策略：1）使用预训练的LLM从原始临床叙述中提取并补齐结构化事件序列；2）从提取的序列中归纳PPDDL概率规划领域模型；3）通过LLM提出的前置条件修订并结合实证验证进行迭代精化，确保模型的预测与实际数据相符。

Result: 在包含2,660份由9位外科医生撰写的腹腔镜阑尾切除术记录的数据集上评估，NSPIN生成的模型能够泛化到未见过的临床记录，专家临床审查确认诱导出的知识总体与标准外科实践一致。

Conclusion: NSPIN证明了神经符号方法能有效克服从非结构化临床文本诱导概率规划模型的挑战，弥合了LLM的语义理解和符号方法的可解释、可验证性之间的鸿沟，为外科工作流决策支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [47] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tydra是一种混合Transformer-SSM架构的表格基础模型，通过交错注意力层和SSM层，在30个OpenML数据集上将推理时间比TabPFN减少30%，同时保持大部分预测性能，并优于大约大10倍的Hydra模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）预测性能优秀但计算复杂度随上下文长度呈平方级增长；而基于SSM的亚二次复杂度替代方案（如Hydra）虽然效率高但在准确性上有所妥协。研究者希望找到一种能在效率和准确性之间取得更好平衡的架构。

Method: 提出Tydra架构，一种混合Transformer-SSM架构，在表格上下文学习中将注意力层和SSM层交错排列，结合Transformer的强预测能力与SSM的亚二次计算效率。

Result: 在30个OpenML数据集上，Tydra相比TabPFN推理时间减少30%且保留大部分预测性能；相比大约大10倍的Hydra模型，Tydra在预测性能上更优且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 混合架构是表格基础模型一个有前景的发展方向，能够在预测准确性和计算效率之间实现更优的权衡。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [48] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAIR（Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine）框架，一种两阶段的生理时间序列插补方法，通过粗插值加多轮循环精炼的方式，结合随机间隔课程学习，在连续血糖监测（AI-READI）和重症监护动脉血压（MIMIC-III）上优于所有基线，且能同时兼顾重建误差与临床负担指标的保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理时间序列（动脉血压、血糖等）的缺失值插补是临床数据处理的关键，但现有通用插补方法在该领域表现不佳——已有基准测试表明简单线性插值在真实临床信号上优于所有学习型插补器。这源于生理缺失的两个特性被通用方法忽视：缺失可能发生在信号临床极端值处（而非典型值），以及缺失间隔长度可能跨越多个数量级。

Method: CAIR采用两阶段框架：第一阶段使用双向GRU插补器生成粗糙的基曲线；第二阶段使用Transformer精炼器在三次连续迭代中反复校正自身估计，使其趋向生理学真实形态。两者在信号无关的随机间隔课程（random-gap curriculum）下联合训练。评估时按间隔长度和缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）分层进行。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III重症动脉血压数据集上，CAIR在所有缺失机制下均实现最高精确度。其相对最强基线的优势随难度增大而扩大：MCAR下为9%，值依赖缺失下为19%（该场景通用学习方法表现最弱）。此外，仅追求低重建误差的插补器无法保留临床医生依赖的负担指标（burden metrics），而CAIR是唯一在重建误差与临床指标保留两个维度均表现最佳的方法。

Conclusion: 生理时间序列插补需要专门设计而非直接套用通用方法；CAIR证明了"先粗插值再逐步精炼"的两阶段范式结合随机间隔课程训练，能有效应对生理缺失的极端值偏向与间隔尺度差异两大挑战，且良好的重建误差并不自动等同于临床指标的保真，二者需同时优化。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [49] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C是一个针对对齐多流遥测数据的可审计严格先验秩校准检测器，结合窗口通道、Fisher聚合与秩排序来检测联合异常。在大不列颠电网六条数据流上的评估中，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排在首位，但通道消融表明该排名来自局部通道而非copula通道；2020年无窗口被选中，归因于记录规则饱和而非无事件。论文强调p值是选择量而非事件概率，copula形式通道并非字面copula密度，经验秩计数是诊断而非覆盖或FDR证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营遥测数据可能存在所有单流均在正常范围内但整体联合异常的情况，现有检测方法难以捕捉此类联合异常。作者希望构建一个可审计的、严格先验秩校准的检测器，能够检测对齐多流遥测中的联合异常，并保持解释的严谨性与可复现性。

Method: TRACE-C采用严格先验秩校准方法：对同一体制的滚动中位数/MAD残差馈入三个窗口通道（最大归一化局部和、基于鲁棒z残差的高斯copula形式依赖对比、最差标准化AR(1)创新），各通道秩通过Fisher聚合再与先前聚合排序比较。评估使用2019年1-4月拟合、2019年7-12月开发证据、2020年冻结保留数据集。所有表格和图形由机器可读报告生成。

Result: TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排在2019测试窗口首位，但消融显示该排名可归因于局部通道（copula-only排第59位）。9月8日短频率事件融合检测器排143位（时间通道单独排40位），重建基线排首位。2020年无窗口被选中，最高排名的冻结窗口后被解读为Storm Ellen。研究揭示了三个解释局限：p值为选择量而非事件概率、copula通道非字面copula密度、经验秩计数为诊断而非覆盖证明。

Conclusion: TRACE-C展示了一种可审计的联合异常检测框架，其对极端事件的排名取决于通道选择，不同异常类型更适合不同通道。2020年的空结果是记录规则饱和而非平静年份。论文强调研究中需严格区分选择量与概率、方法命名与实际操作的差异，以及经验诊断与形式保证的界限。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [50] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架，通过大语言模型自动生成概念及标注规则，将预测组织为三个互补的概念瓶颈（历史上下文、局部预测区间、完整预测视野），在保持与黑盒基线相当的预测精度的同时，提供了语义可解释且支持概念级干预的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的多元时间序列预测器能够建模复杂的时序和跨变量依赖，但其不透明的表示无法提供关于预测依据的洞察，限制了在需要理解预测背后因素的实务场景中的应用。

Method: ConceptTS使用大语言模型提出与任务相关的概念并生成可执行的标注规则，将语言模型的领域知识转化为直接监督，无需昂贵的人工概念标注。概念被组织为三个互补的瓶颈层：历史上下文、局部预测区间和完整预测视野。共享解码器结合预测的激活表示来构建预测，使决策过程显式化并支持概念级干预。

Result: 在北京多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS在预测精度上与强黑盒基线相当，同时产生语义有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: ConceptTS在保持竞争力的预测准确度的同时，通过概念化的表示使多变量时间序列预测具备可解释性，为在需要理解预测依据的场景中部署高性能预测模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [51] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARCL，一种用于解析持续学习（analytic continual learning）的谱分区方法。它通过将自相关矩阵分解为高能核心和残差补集，冻结旧类别在核心子空间中的分类器分量，仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，从而解决解析方法中由谱干扰（spectral interference）导致的旧类别漂移问题。在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上使用冻结ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL大幅缩小了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析持续学习方法虽以闭式岭更新替代迭代优化，避免了随机梯度覆盖导致的遗忘叙事，但实际上仍存在旧类别的漂移问题，且其原因不同于梯度方法。作者识别出根本原因是谱干扰：所有任务的联合岭分类器共享逆自相关算子(R+λI)^{-1}，新任务样本在旧主导特征方向上加载时会稀释谱并扰动旧类logits。已有方法无法解释和解决这一机制。

Method: SPARCL将运行自相关分解为高能量核心子空间和残差补集两部分，冻结旧类别分类器在核心子空间中的分量，仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，并可选地加入残差随机投影扩展。更新过程为简单的闭式形式，且对旧logits的核心贡献具有可证明的不变性保证。

Result: 在冻结ViT-B/16协议下，SPARCL在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A四个基准上，几乎弥合了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法（如表示匹配类方法）之间的性能差距，并且与稀疏特征去相关方法（如Fly-CL）互补。

Conclusion: SPARCL通过谱分区策略有效缓解了解析持续学习中的谱干扰问题，为理解解析方法中的遗忘机制提供了新的视角，同时证明了该方法在多个基准上具有显著优势，并可与现有特征去相关方法互补结合。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [52] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: E²-TTT通过推导闭式状态转移，在保持逐token递归时间结构的同时实现完全并行化的chunk级训练，在1.3B参数规模下与基线性能相当或更优，并在长度外推和上下文检索任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时训练（TTT）方法在处理长上下文时，难以在逐token更新动力学的表达力与chunk级近似的硬件效率之间取得平衡。当前的chunk-wise方法丢弃了更新规则的时间结构，导致表达力损失。

Method: 在chunk-start权重处取梯度的标准近似下，推导出闭式状态转移公式，精确复现逐token递归的chunk-end快权重和动量状态。这使得chunk级训练可以完全并行化，同时保留prior chunk-wise方法所丢弃的更新规则时间结构。

Result: 从零训练至1.3B参数的模型，在语言建模上与先前TTT和混合注意力基线持平，在上下文检索上超越它们。在长度外推的"NIH"测试中，在8倍训练上下文长度下保持超过90%的准确率。训练吞吐量与高效的chunk-wise方法相当。

Conclusion: E²-TTT成功弥合了表达力与效率之间的差距，证明可以在保留逐token递归表达力的同时实现高效并行化训练，为长上下文处理提供了一种兼顾性能与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [53] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Time-Aware Transformer的AECOPD预测模型，利用日常呼吸机产生的呼吸数据而非传统临床和实验室数据，通过时间感知的Transformer架构捕捉症状及其时间进展，在多个分类任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，需要具有时间敏感性的预测模型。然而现有机器学习模型多依赖临床和实验室数据，不可避免地引入延迟。为实现在家庭监测场景下对AECOPD的及时检测并最小化延迟，需要利用日常呼吸机产生的呼吸数据进行预测。

Method: 提出一种Time-Aware的Transformer-based AECOPD预测模型，通过Time-Aware Transformer生成有意义的患者表征，以捕捉呼吸机数据中的症状及其时间进展信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，该Time-Aware Transformer方法在多个分类任务中优于传统方法，展示了其提升AECOPD预测准确率的潜力。

Conclusion: 基于时间感知Transformer的模型能够利用家庭呼吸机数据有效预测AECOPD，较传统方法具有更优的性能，有望提升AECOPD预测的准确性和及时性。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [54] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究短观测定价面板中"大量观测但少数价格变动"这一特性对统计推断的影响。通过校准到稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，作者区分了条件于已实现价格轨迹的不确定性与跨替代轨迹的估计误差变化。基线模拟中，后者（跨设计分量）占梯度提升模型估计误差方差的97.6%。此外，作者建立了经验关系σ̂≈0.182V^(-0.271)，发现Paule-Mandel方差分量估计可将同质模拟中的经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短观测面板可能包含大量观测值但只有极少数的离散价格变动，这种"观测多而变动少"的特性会产生严重的推断后果，而传统推断方法往往忽略了不同价格轨迹之间的设计层面不确定性。作者希望系统研究这种系统性不确定性对估计误差的影响。

Method: 使用校准到稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程（DGP），将不确定性分解为条件于已实现价格轨迹的确定性分量与跨替代轨迹的跨设计分量。通过基线模拟量化两分量占比，采用梯度提升模型，并引入经验标度关系σ̂≈0.182V^(-0.271)（V=价格变动次数×变动幅度的平方）描述跨设计离散度。进一步通过模拟比较Paule-Mandel方差分量估计与面板内重抽样方法的表现。

Result: 基线模拟中跨设计分量占估计误差方差的97.6%；跨设计离散度满足经验关系σ̂≈0.182V^(-0.271)；共享价格路径的区域不能增加独立的价格轨迹，而跨独立单位的平均可按标准平方根速率降低离散度；Paule-Mandel方法在独立定价单位间估计方差分量，将同质模拟的经验覆盖率从0.469提升到0.931。

Conclusion: 核心启示是研究设计应转向构建能够产生独立识别变异的DGP，而非仅仅依赖固定的被动面板。面板内重抽样方法因仅利用单条已实现轨迹的信息而无法识别跨设计不确定性分量。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [55] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对自我精炼（self-refinement）流水线中的生成、批判、修订三个阶段进行模型规模的分阶段系统研究，基于Qwen3的6种规模和Gemma 3的4种规模在5个跨领域基准上进行实验，发现生成器和修订器的规模越大效果越好，但过小的修订器反而有害；而批判器的规模对性能影响极不敏感，但即使很小的批判器也优于完全省略批判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我精炼范式（生成-批判-修订）在LLM中广泛应用，三个阶段具有不同的认知需求，但现有方法通常将模型规模视为实现细节而非研究对象，导致资源浪费。缺乏对模型规模如何影响每个阶段以及是否各阶段都需要同等能力的模型的系统研究。

Method: 采用分阶段（stage-wise）模型规模研究方法，在5个不同领域的基准上，使用Qwen3的6种模型规模和Gemma 3的4种模型规模，对自我精炼流水线的生成、批判、修订三个阶段分别进行独立的模型规模控制实验，系统地比较不同规模组合对输出质量的影响。

Result: 关键发现：(1) 更大的生成器和修订器一般能提升流水线性能，但规模不足的修订器甚至可能损害性能；(2) 批判器的规模对整体性能高度不敏感，但即使包含一个小型批判器也能持续优于完全省略批判步骤。模型容量不应在流水线各阶段均匀分配，不同阶段展现出不同的规模扩展特征。

Conclusion: 自我精炼流水线中各阶段对模型规模的需求存在显著差异，容量分配不应是均匀的。该研究为设计计算效率更高的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导，表明在资源受限情况下优先扩大生成器和修订器规模、使用小型批判器即可取得较好的性能-效率权衡。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>

### [56] [Scalpel3: A High-Performance Data Carving Architecture for Recovery of Fragmented Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20363)
*Karley Waguespack,Samuel Goodwin,George Hendrick,Samuel Hildebrand,Thomas Landaiche,Mingyang Li,Joshua McCain,Tyler Saizan,Jacob Tucker,James M. Ghawaly,Golden G. Richard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Scalpel3是一个大规模多线程的文件雕刻框架，支持连续和碎片化文件恢复，允许研究人员仅编写单线程的验证和重组代码即可部署新的恢复策略，并集成ONNX Runtime支持学习模型，在超过80,000个文件的语料库上验证了其高效率和高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文件雕刻工具只能有效恢复连续文件，而缺乏一个公开可用、格式无关、高性能的框架，供研究人员开发和部署新的碎片化恢复策略。

Method: 提出Scalpel3架构，采用大规模多线程设计，将工作调度、同步、检查点和I/O等基础设施与单线程的验证和重组代码分离；集成ONNX Runtime以支持学习模型用于验证器和恢复策略；提供交互式人工干预、块去重、持久化重启检查点、增量输出及FUSE文件系统等操作特性。

Result: 在包含超过80,000个文件的混合语料库上，评估了连续恢复和三种受控碎片化场景（间隙、乱序块放置、两者结合），结果表明连续恢复快速且准确，多线程架构使碎片化验证结果显著早于单线程执行，针对特定文件类型的定制策略在不同难度布局下保持高整体准确率。

Conclusion: Scalepel3为大规模开发和部署碎片化恢复策略提供了实用基础，填补了格式无关的高性能文件雕刻框架的空白。

Abstract: File carving recovers files from raw storage without filesystem metadata, a key capability in digital forensics, data recovery, and digital exploration. Existing tools recover contiguous files effectively, but, to our knowledge, no publicly available, format-agnostic, high-performance framework exists in which researchers can develop and deploy new fragmented recovery strategies. Scalpel3 fills this gap with a massively threaded architecture for contiguous and fragmented recovery. Researchers need only write single-threaded validation and reassembly code for a new file type; Scalpel3 supplies worker scheduling, synchronization, checkpointing, and I/O. This separation allows new recovery methods to be added without modifying the backend infrastructure. The architecture also integrates the ONNX Runtime, allowing learned models to be used within validators and recovery strategies. Operational features include interactive human-in-the-loop control, block deduplication, persistent restart checkpoints, incremental output, and a FUSE filesystem for hybrid workflows.
  We evaluate Scalpel3 on a mixed corpus of more than 80,000 files under contiguous recovery and three controlled fragmentation scenarios: gaps, out-of-order block placement, and both together. Results show fast and accurate contiguous recovery and demonstrate that Scalpel3's massively threaded architecture makes validated fragmented results available substantially earlier than single-threaded execution. Furthermore, strategies tailored to individual file types maintain high overall accuracy across increasingly difficult layouts. Together, these results demonstrate that Scalpel3 provides a practical foundation for developing and deploying fragmented recovery strategies at scale.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking LLM Serving Systems for Agentic AI Workloads with XPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20370)
*Michael Wang,Yikang Yue,Shaobo Li,Yirui Eric Zhou,Chen Wang,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: XPerf是一个用于对LLM服务系统进行代理型AI工作负载压力测试的基准测试框架，采用细粒度轨迹重放方法最小化工作负载变异，支持从真实应用中收集轨迹、合成新工作负载并可重现地在不同服务系统上重放，内置8个代理应用，可提供详细的系统与硬件性能剖析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型AI工作负载依赖于非确定性的LLM输出来驱动控制流，导致工作负载模式在每次运行时不可预测地变化，使得在代理工作负载下对LLM服务系统进行基准测试具有挑战性。

Method: XPerf采用细粒度轨迹重放方法：用户可从真实代理应用中收集轨迹，按需合成具有不同模式的新工作负载，并在不同LLM服务系统上可重现地重放这些轨迹，以最小化工作负载变异。框架内置8个覆盖多样用例（如编码、深度研究、问答）的代理应用，并提供服务系统与硬件的详细剖析。

Result: 实验研究表明XPerf能够准确重放代理工作负载，提供详细的性能分解结果，可扩展到更大的服务系统，并能辅助服务系统调试。

Conclusion: XPerf通过轨迹重放解决了代理型工作负载基准测试中非确定性问题，为LLM服务系统在代理工作负载下的性能评估提供了可靠且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: We present XPerf, a benchmarking framework that load-tests LLM serving systems with diverse agentic AI workloads. It provides detailed profiling of the serving system and hardware, enabling users to identify performance bottlenecks introduced by agentic workloads. Benchmarking LLM serving systems under agentic workloads is challenging - agentic applications rely on nondeterministic LLM outputs to guide their control flow; therefore, workload patterns vary unpredictably from run to run. XPerf minimizes this workload variation with a fine-grained trace replay approach: it enables users to easily collect traces from real agentic applications, synthesize new workloads with various patterns if needed, and reproducibly replay them on different LLM serving systems. XPerf includes eight agentic applications across diverse use cases (e.g., coding, deep research, and Q&A) by default. Our empirical study using these workloads shows that XPerf accurately replays agentic workloads, provides detailed performance breakdowns, scales to larger serving systems, and assists in serving system debugging. We will open-source XPerf on GitHub.

</details>


### [58] [Enabling Memory-efficient Im2win Convolution with Multi-precision Support on GPU CUDA and Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20725)
*Xiang Fu,Jixiang Ma,Xinpeng Zhang,Peng Zhao,Shuai Lu,Xu Tony Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了im2win卷积范式，使其能同时在CUDA核心（全精度）和Tensor Core（半精度）上高效运行，通过新内核设计、zig-zag内存访问和异步数据移动等优化，在十二个CNN基准上相比cuDNN最高提升1.4x TFLOPS，相比GEMM卷积提升6.4x，同时大幅降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积是深度神经网络中的主要计算瓶颈，其效率高度依赖算法与GPU硬件的紧耦合。现有的GPU卷积方法存在内存开销大、缓存利用率差、对不同核大小适应性有限以及数值不稳定等问题，亟需一种在多种精度和硬件单元上都能高效运行的统一卷积方案。

Method: 本文基于im2win范式，将其从CUDA核心扩展到Tensor Core上运行半精度计算。具体引入了新的内核设计、zig-zag内存访问模式以提升缓存利用率，以及异步数据移动等优化手段，从而有效利用硬件加速的半精度矩阵乘累加操作。

Result: 在十二个CNN基准测试中，im2win相比其自身CUDA核心实现提升最高2.8x TFLOPS，相比cuDNN提升1.4x，相比基于GEMM的cuBLAS卷积提升6.4x；同时内存占用分别仅为后两者的53%和35%。

Conclusion: im2win被证实是一个适用于现代GPU架构的统一高性能卷积框架，兼具全精度与半精度支持、内存高效和跨核大小通用性的优势。

Abstract: Convolution is a principal computational bottleneck in deep neural networks, and its efficiency depends on tight integration between algorithms and GPU hardware. Existing GPU convolution methods suffer from large memory overhead, poor cache utilization, limited effectiveness across kernel sizes, or numerical instability. This work extends the im2win paradigm -- a universal, memory-efficient convolution method with contiguous memory access for all kernel sizes -- to run efficiently in full precision on CUDA cores and half precision on tensor cores. By introducing new kernel designs and optimizations such as zig-zag memory access and asynchronous data movement, im2win efficiently exploits hardware-accelerated half-precision matrix multiply-accumulate operations. Across twelve CNN benchmarks, im2win achieves up to 2.8x higher TFLOPS than its CUDA core implementation, 1.4x higher than cuDNN, and 6.4x higher than GEMM-based convolution with cuBLAS, while using as little as 53% and 35% of their memory, respectively. These results establish im2win as a unified, high-performance convolution framework for modern GPU architectures.

</details>


### [59] [MEMPOWER: Efficient Power Management with Fine-grained Memory Analysis and Modeling for HPC Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20734)
*Nanda Velugoti,Joseph Manzano,Andres Marquez,Nathan Tallent,Kyle Hale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMPOWER，一种基于模型的灵活方法，通过暴露并行工作负载中细粒度的计算/数据移动不平衡，静态插桩应用二进制文件以自动执行电压/频率转换，在HPC基准测试上实现6%-42%的EDP降低，且对执行时间影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC和云环境中并行应用的能耗与功率效率管理至关重要，但现有频率/电压缩放技术无法捕捉细粒度的内存访问行为变化，导致计算/数据访问不平衡对系统不可见，白白浪费了潜在的节能机会。

Method: MEMPOWER采用基于模型的方法来刻画并行工作负载的细粒度内存行为，进而驱动自动化软件框架对应用二进制进行静态插桩，注入由模型确定的电压/频率转换点，以在细粒度内存访问行为变化与硬件转换成本之间取得平衡。

Result: 在一系列HPC基准测试上，相比标准的OS/硬件管理的功耗控制机制，MEMPOWER将EDP（能量延迟积）降低了6%至42%，同时对执行时间的影响极小。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度地暴露计算/数据移动不平衡并利用静态插桩进行自动电压/频率调节，MEMPOWER在保持运行时间基本不受影响的同时，显著提升了并行工作负载的能效，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Managing the energy consumption and power efficiency of parallel applications is a significant issue in both HPC environments and in the cloud. As emerging applications continue to push against the memory wall of modern machines, the growing imbalance between compute and data movement creates new opportunities to intelligently tune CPU power consumption. Unfortunately, existing frequency and voltage scaling techniques do not adequately capture fine-grained changes in memory access behavior, rendering the compute/data access imbalance invisible to the components of the system that could capitalize on it, thus leaving potential power savings on the table. In this paper, we propose MEMPOWER, a flexible, model-based approach to exposing compute/data movement imbalance that characterizes the fine-grained memory behavior of parallel workloads. This characterization then informs our automated software framework which can statically instrument the application binary with model-determined voltage/frequency transitions that balance fine-grained changes in memory access behavior with the costs of hardware transitions. Using MEMPOWER, we demonstrate a reduction in EDP of 6% to 42% on a range of HPC benchmarks with minimal impact on execution time when compared to the standard OS/hardware-managed power control mechanism.

</details>


### [60] [PRICE: Pricing-based Resource Incentives for Quality-of-Result-aware Computing at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20819)
*Uwe Gropengießer,Sebastian Frenz,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PRICE是一种边缘节点激励机制，通过将利用率依赖的价格信号与每个请求的质量选择耦合，在过载时通过价格上升促使请求选择更轻量的执行变体，从而在容量受限的边缘节点上显著提高可服务请求数量和CPU利用率，且在不同定价函数、任务时长分布和客户端群体下均表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘节点在设计中容量受限，然而许多边缘工作负载可以在运行时以结果质量换取资源效率。现有的边缘定价机制大多将请求视为固定配置提交，很少在过载情况下利用每个请求的质量灵活性，这一控制维度尚未被充分开发。

Method: 提出PRICE激励机制，将利用率依赖的价格信号与每请求质量选择耦合。当利用率上升时，接受价格上涨，使得资源密集型的请求变体被选中的可能性降低，将接受的请求转向更轻量的执行方式，从而在接近容量极限的情况下服务更多请求。在真实硬件上持续过载条件下进行了评估。

Result: 在持续过载的真实硬件评估中，PRICE在接受的吞吐量和CPU利用率方面均优于固定分配和动态定价基线。结果对定价函数族、任务持续时间分布和客户端群体均表现稳健。

Conclusion: 结果质量灵活性是边缘过载管理中被低估且强大的控制维度，而定价是有效利用该维度的机制。

Abstract: Edge nodes are capacity-constrained by design, yet many edge workloads can trade result quality for resource efficiency at runtime. Existing edge pricing mechanisms largely treat requests as fixed-configuration submissions and rarely exploit per-request quality flexibility under overload. We present PRICE, an incentive mechanism that couples a utilization-dependent price signal to per-request quality selection. As utilization increases, rising acceptance prices make resource-intensive variants less likely to be selected, shifting accepted requests toward lighter execution and allowing the node to serve significantly more requests while operating near capacity. Evaluation on real hardware under sustained overload shows that PRICE outperforms both fixed-allocation and dynamic-pricing baselines in accepted throughput and CPU utilization. The results are robust across pricing function families, task-duration distributions, and client populations. Result-quality flexibility is a powerful but underused control dimension for overload management at the edge, and pricing is an effective mechanism to exploit it.

</details>


### [61] [AI Infrastructure in Space: How Far Can We Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21034)
*Qing Li,Qiyang Zhang,Daliang Xu,Tianze Huang,Dingge Zhang,Yihao Zhao,Xiaolong Huang,Jinfeng Wen,Xiameng Hu,Tao Qi,Mengwei Xu,Shangguang Wang,Xuanzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对太空AI基础设施的系统愿景，将轨道和物理状态纳入资源模型，通过BUPT-1和BUPT-2卫星的三项在轨案例验证了热/能量约束对计算能力的限制、应用更新传输的优化效果以及状态恢复成为关键系统问题，并据此提出了空间原生资源管理、生命周期支持和跨星地持续AI服务的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星正逐渐成为可编程计算平台，但轨道时间、连通性、能量和热余量随轨道运行不断变化，使得AI服务在发射后难以保持可部署、可管理和可恢复。现有研究缺乏一个系统化的视角来统一理解和管理这些挑战，因此需要建立一个太空AI基础设施的系统愿景。

Method: 作者通过三方面展开研究：(1) 综合提炼地面AI基础设施、卫星组网和卫星边缘计算的相关基础；(2) 分析直接影响系统设计的物理约束条件；(3) 在BUPT-1和BUPT-2卫星上开展三个层次（节点层、平台层、服务层）的在轨案例研究，包括热/能量受限下的可用计算能力测量、应用更新传输优化（SateLight方案）以及有状态VLM服务的执行状态恢复。

Result: BUPT-1卫星遥测显示可用计算能力受热和能量包络限制；BUPT-2卫星上的SateLight方案将应用更新传输延迟平均降低56.54%，最高降低91.18%，并实现100%的更新正确性；有状态VLM服务案例表明热中断使执行状态恢复成为一级系统问题。

Conclusion: 轨道和物理状态应被当作AI系统资源模型的核心组成部分。未来的研究应聚焦于空间原生的资源管理、生命周期支持机制，以及如何在地面-太空之间实现可持续的AI服务保障。

Abstract: Satellites are becoming programmable computing platforms capable of running increasingly demanding AI workloads. This shift raises a systems problem: how can AI services remain deployable, manageable, and recoverable after launch when compute capacity, connectivity, energy, and thermal headroom vary over orbital time? This paper develops a systems vision for AI infrastructure in space. We define it as the systems layer that manages AI capabilities across spacecraft, orbital networks, ground stations, and cloud backends, while treating orbital and physical state as part of the resource model. We synthesize relevant foundations from terrestrial AI infrastructure, satellite networking, and satellite edge computing, and examine the physical constraints that directly shape system design. We further ground this vision in three in-orbit case studies spanning the node, platform, and service levels. Telemetry from BUPT-1 satellite shows that usable compute capacity is bounded by thermal and energy envelopes. SateLight on BUPT-2 satellite reduces application-update transmission latency by 56.54% on average and up to 91.18%, with 100% update correctness. A stateful VLM serving case further shows that thermal interruptions make execution-state recovery a first-class systems problem. These observations motivate a research agenda for space-native resource management, lifecycle support, and sustained AI service across space and ground.

</details>


### [62] [Integrating a Python Dynamical core into ICON](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21150)
*Mauro Bianco,Till Ehrengruber,Enrique González Paredes,Andreas Jocksch,Christos Kotsalos,Ioannis Magkanaris,Philip Müller,Edoardo Paone,Mikael Simberg,Hannes Vogt,Jacopo Canton,Yilu Chen,Anurag Dipankar,Nicoletta Farabullini,Michael Jähn,Matthieu Leclair,Ong Chia Rui,Nathan Beech,Nicolas Gruber,Christoph Müller,Daniel Hupp,Xavier Lapillonne*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文展示了将基于Python的ICON动力核心集成到传统Fortran仿真代码中，借助GT4Py DSL和DaCe优化框架，在不损失性能的前提下实现无缝集成，并在全球气候模拟中比高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现提升20-30%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统模型向百亿亿次级计算迁移常受制于僵化的Fortran代码库和高维护成本的编译器指令。虽然高级DSL提供了解决方案，但集成过程繁琐导致失败率很高。

Method: 将Python-based ICON动力核心集成到原始Fortran仿真代码中，利用GT4Py领域特定语言构建Python代码，并通过Data-Centric (DaCe)优化框架实现数据流优化和自动化内核融合，从单一的便携Python源码生成优化的设备代码。

Result: 在全球生产级仿真中，Python动力核心相比高度优化的Fortran+OpenACC实现有20-30%的性能提升，在耦合配置下整体时间提升10%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明Python可以摆脱对硬件的直接依赖，为全球气候建模提供可持续、高效且硬件无关的未来路径，挑战了Python编排会引入难以接受的高性能计算开销的普遍假设。

Abstract: The transition of Earth-system models to exascale is often hindered by rigid, monolithic Fortran codebases and maintenance-heavy compiler directives. While high-level DSLs offer a solution, they frequently fail due to cumbersome integration. We present the integration of a Python-based ICON dynamical core into the original Fortran simulation code. Leveraging the GT4Py DSL and the Data-Centric (DaCe) optimization framework, we demonstrate that high-level Python can be seamlessly integrated into legacy infrastructure without performance loss. Our results challenge the assumption that Python orchestration introduces prohibitive HPC overhead. In production-grade global simulations, our Python dynamical core achieves a 20--30\% performance improvement over the highly-optimized Fortran+OpenACC implementation, with a 10\% improvement on the total time for a coupled setup. Driven by advanced data-flow optimizations and automated kernel fusion, this approach replaces hardware-entangled directives by generating optimized device code from a single, portable Python source. This work proves that Python can provide a sustainable, efficient, and hardware-agnostic future for global climate modeling.

</details>


### [63] [HIERA: Workload-Aware Planning Across Implementation Spaces for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21157)
*Jinghao Wang,Qiqi Gu,Chenpeng Wu,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HIERA是一个用于GPU内核优化的分层搜索空间规划框架，通过构建契约增强的任务规格说明，在不同实现空间（PyTorch算子、CUDA库、自定义CUDA内核）之间进行选择和结构化迭代精化，在KernelBench上超越现有无训练方法，并与有训练的CUDA-L1方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的GPU内核优化方法通常在固定的实现空间内优化，这限制了优化灵活性或搜索效率。随着工作负载日益多样化和GPU硬件快速发展，亟需一种能够自动在多个实现空间中规划搜索路径的高效方法。

Method: HIERA的核心方法包括：(1) 构建契约增强的任务规格说明（contract-augmented task specifications）；(2) 在PyTorch算子、CUDA库和自定义CUDA内核等多个实现空间中选择合适的搜索空间；(3) 利用性能分析反馈和专家知识引导结构化的迭代精化过程。

Result: 在KernelBench多个工作负载级别和多种基础LLM上的实验表明，HIERA在实现有效性、样本效率和优化性能方面均优于现有无训练方法，且与需要额外模型训练的CUDA-L1方法竞争力相当。在科学计算领域的专用stencil算子案例中，实现了对比cuDNN的1.53倍加速。

Conclusion: HIERA展示了分层搜索空间规划在GPU内核优化中的有效性，不仅适用于标准机器学习工作负载，还能推广到科学计算等更广泛的领域，证明了通用框架的潜力。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels underpin modern deep learning and scientific computing. As workloads become increasingly diverse and GPU hardware evolves rapidly, developing efficient methods for automated GPU kernel generation and optimization has become increasingly important. Existing LLM-based methods typically optimize within a fixed implementation space, limiting either optimization flexibility or search efficiency. We propose \textsc{HIERA}, a hierarchical search-space planning framework for GPU kernel optimization. \textsc{HIERA} constructs contract-augmented task specifications, selects an appropriate implementation space across PyTorch operators, CUDA libraries, and custom CUDA kernels, and uses profiling feedback and expert knowledge to guide structured iterative refinement. Experiments on KernelBench across multiple various workload levels and base LLMs show that \textsc{HIERA} delivers stronger overall implementation validity, sample efficiency, and optimization performance than existing training-free methods, while remaining competitive with the training-based CUDA-L1 without additional model training. A case study on a specialized stencil operator from scientific computing further achieves a \(1.53\times\) speedup over cuDNN, demonstrating the potentiality of the general framework beyond standard machine-learning workloads.

</details>


### [64] [GrAND: GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21163)
*Karthik Venkatasubba,Shivendra Deshpande,Shivram S,Jyothi Vedurada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GrAND，一套GPU原生的动态图索引更新算法集合，支持Vamana和CAGRA两种流行图索引的在线插入与删除，通过批量图修复、无锁查找替换策略和按需反向图构建，实现了2.2x-8.7x（对比SVFusion）和6.5x-25.4x（对比FreshDiskANN-GPU）的吞吐量提升，同时保持高搜索吞吐量和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ANNS应用需要处理持续演化的向量集合，要求图索引在高吞吐搜索的同时支持高频插入和删除操作。然而现有的GPU图索引大多是静态的或仅提供有限的更新支持，因为图更新涉及邻居发现、反向边创建、剪枝和删除引发的图修复，这些并发操作会引入大量冗余距离计算和共享邻接表的冲突访问。基于后台重建的删除策略还会带来显著的计算开销、额外内存消耗以及对前台查询的干扰。

Method: GrAND采用三个核心技术：(1) 批量整合图修复，消除冗余的剪枝计算；(2) 无锁的查找-替换策略实现并行邻接表更新，避免锁竞争；(3) 在GPU上按需构建反向图，准确识别入边以支持可靠的原地删除，而不需要永久复制索引结构。GrAND将这些算法应用于Vamana和CAGRA两种主流图索引。

Result: 在七个真实世界数据集上的五种流式工作负载中评估，GrAND相比SVFusion整体工作负载吞吐量提升2.2x-8.7x，相比FreshDiskANN-GPU提升6.5x-25.4x，同时在持续更新过程中保持了高搜索吞吐量和召回率。

Conclusion: GrAND证明了GPU图索引可以在不牺牲搜索性能的前提下实现高效的动态更新，通过消除冗余计算、避免锁竞争和按需反向图构建等设计，显著提升了动态ANNS的整体工作负载吞吐量，为GPU上的动态图索引设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Modern Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search (ANNS) applications operate over continuously evolving vector collections and require graph indexes that sustain high-throughput searches while incorporating insertions and deletions with high recall. However, most GPU graph indexes are static or provide limited update support. Updates require neighbour discovery, reverse-edge creation, pruning, and deletion-induced graph repair; executing these operations concurrently introduces redundant distance computations and conflicting accesses to shared adjacency lists. Background-rebuild-based deletion further incurs substantial computation, additional memory consumption, and interference with foreground queries.
  We present GrAND (GPU-based Dynamic Graph Indexes for Approximate Nearest Neighbour Search), a GPU-native collection of dynamic-update algorithms for two popular graph indexes, Vamana and CAGRA. GrAND consolidates graph repair across a batch, eliminating redundant pruning computations, and employs a lock-free find-and-replace strategy for parallel adjacency-list updates. For reliable in-place deletion, GrAND constructs an on-demand reverse graph on the GPU, accurately identifying incoming edges without permanently duplicating the index. We evaluate GrAND on seven real-world datasets across five streaming workloads, comparing it against SVFusion and FreshDiskANN-GPU (our GPU adaptation of FreshDiskANN). GrAND improves overall workload throughput by 2.2x-8.7x and 6.5x-25.4x, respectively, while maintaining high search throughput and recall over sustained updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [65] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范驱动的智能体开发"(SDAD)范式，将传统软件开发纪律前移至规格化阶段，通过意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体综合和多智能体验证来支撑千亿级LLM上下文窗口下的自主交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM上下文窗口从数十万扩展到百万token，前沿编码智能体正在重构软件开发生命周期(SDLC)，使规范质量成为自主交付的执行燃料，但现有Waterfall与Agile范式无法充分应对这一转变，需要新的形式化模型。

Method: 论文形式化SDAD框架，包括五个核心阶段（意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体综合、独立多智能体验证、人工签核）；引入"AI代码"作为第四种生产范式；对比Human-Agile与Agentic-SDAD在工件、节奏、问责制与安全态势上的差异；并提出量化治理指标（歧义税、规范保真度、SER、带修复乘数phi的TCI_agentic）及混合同步估算与分阶段迁移蓝图。

Result: 论文整合了工业与研究证据表明AI增强测试和验证应分离综合与发布权，论证智能体速度不会消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上移至规格精确性、显式门控和可审计溯源。

Conclusion: SDAD将开发纪律从"实现阶段"重新定位到"规格化阶段"，以支撑规模化的自主交付，同时保持人类签核作为最终问责所在。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [66] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAO是一个为LLM编码代理预加载记忆的系统，通过并行查询PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引，以文件系统注入方式将编译好的简报交付给新启动的Claude Code实例，并在四个月的部署中经历了三代上下文交付机制的迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话都从空上下文窗口开始，丢弃了先前工作中积累的知识，导致跨会话的持续学习和上下文保留能力缺失。

Method: PAO在Claude Code实例启动时并行查询两个独立的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），使用后端特定的检索策略融合结果，通过利用主机代理配置自动读取行为的文件系统注入方式交付编译简报，并管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预播种、就绪轮询与错误检测，以及自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 该系统在2025年12月至2026年3月的四个月常规部署中运行，作为经验报告记录了三种上下文交付机制、每种重新设计所驱动的失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: PAO验证了通过记忆预加载增强LLM编码代理跨会话能力的可行性，并提供了关于多记忆后端融合、文件系统注入策略和代理生命周期管理的实践经验与工程教训。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [67] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了LLM安全对齐的表面性——拒绝机制仅作用于生成末端而未根除预训练中的有害知识。通过分析三个SLM家族在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹，发现了"意图视界"（约15-20%层深度），并提出轻量级探测防御LIV，在PKU-SafeRLHF上比标准护栏提升20-50%的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的安全对齐往往是表面性的，拒绝机制只在生成最后阶段触发，并未真正消除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识。这种架构上的脱节使得模型容易受到"语义伪装"攻击——将有害意图包裹在良性叙事语境中，从而绕过标准的输入输出护栏。

Method: 1) 分析三种SLM家族（Phi-3、Qwen2.5、Gemma-2b）在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹；2) 识别出普遍的"意图视界"现象——在约15-20%总层深度处，模型预训练表征的有害意图随上下文安全化而坍缩；3) 提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)，一种利用早期层保留的"危害签名"进行探测的轻量级防御方法。

Result: 晚期层对伪装攻击的表征与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检测率<20%），但早期层保留了可检测的"危害签名"。LIV在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上的所有测试架构中，比标准护栏高出20-50%的检测率，且无需模型重训练即可有效中和零日语义攻击。

Conclusion: 安全对齐需要从生成末端的拒绝机制转向对模型内部潜在表征的深入理解与利用。通过在更早期层进行意图验证，可以显著提升对语义伪装攻击的检测能力，且无需昂贵的模型重训练，为构建更鲁棒的LLM安全防御提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [68] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性综述了多模态在智能体（Agent）框架中的核心作用，从感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动五个功能模块出发，分析了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演化过程，总结了委托式、晚期融合和早期融合三种模态集成架构，并梳理了机器人、GUI与网页导航、多模态内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索等应用领域中的多模态智能体系统及性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有基于大语言模型的智能体综述，但近年来缺乏对多模态如何塑造智能体能力的系统性考察。随着大型多模态模型（LMMs）的发展，智能体能够处理图像、音频和视频等多模态数据，提升真实世界适用性，因此需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用以模态为中心的视角，围绕智能体框架的五个核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）进行分析；通过委托式（delegated）、晚期融合（late-fusion）和早期融合（early-fusion）三种架构分类考察模态集成方式；以模态中心分类法将架构设计选择与智能体能力相关联；并在多应用领域中进行系统性回顾。

Result: 梳理了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演化路径，识别了不同领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多模态内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）中多模态智能体系统的能力表现，分析了训练与推理成本、延迟和部署约束等效率-可扩展性权衡。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，识别了关键研究空白，并为构建稳健、通用的智能系统绘制了路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [69] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（Tree-based Ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持可解释性的同时提升准确率，解决了Transformer模型在高性能但不可解释方面的缺陷。所提方法在IEMOCAP和CMU-MOSI数据集上分别比Multimodal Transformer提升F1-mod 4.3%，比可解释基线IMR提升准确率3.0%，且人机标注一致性显著优于默认特征重要性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感与行为分类器（融合文本、音频、视觉）需要在获得高精度的同时提供人类可理解的可解释性解释。然而，当前高性能模型（尤其是Transformer）虽预测能力强，但其分布式表示与深层非线性结构难以对单个多模态特征分配有意义的权重，限制了其在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。

Method: 提出基于树集成的可解释多模态分类框架：(1) 将每个模态编码为token；(2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；(3) 通过树集成分类器路由融合多模态数据；(4) 使用改进的特征重要性度量（modified feature importance）解释趋势，该度量降低二分类中负类的影响，从而改进指示器/标记检测能力。提出的具体模型包括线性判别树（LDT）、线性判别森林（LDF）和线性判别AdaBoost（LDAB）。

Result: 所提树集成模型相比Multimodal Transformer获得4.3%的F1-mod增益，比主要可解释多模态基线（IMR）获得3.0%的准确率增益。此外，提出的多模态特征重要性方法提取的跨模态显著概念的人机标注一致性大幅高于默认特征重要性：在IEMOCAP上为62.2% vs 43.2%，在CMU-MOSI上为46.7% vs 32.1%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了树集成方法能够在多模态情感和行为分类中同时实现竞争性准确率与强可解释性，填补了高容量模型（如Transformer）在信任敏感应用场景下可解释性不足的空白。改进的特征重要性指标和人机标注一致性结果表明，该框架在临床情感监测和教育评估等应用中有重要潜力。
